z_0 (weight 1) = (2*d_aa - 2*d_bb) + i (0)
z_1 (weight 1) = (2*d_ap - 2*d_bp) + i (0)
z_2 (weight 1) = (2*e_a - 2*e_b) + i (0)
inv[0]  (2 terms)  =  d_aa + d_bb
inv[1]  (1 terms)  =  2*d_pp
inv[2]  (1 terms)  =  2*d_ab
inv[3]  (2 terms)  =  d_ap + d_bp
